Always include actual file paths formatted with backticks: `src/services/user.ts`.\n28\t</philosophy>\n29\t\n30\t<process>\n31\t\n32\t<step name=\"parse_paths_flag\" priority=\"first\">\n33\tParse an optional `--paths <p1,p2,...>` argument. When supplied (by the\n34\tpost-execute codebase-drift gate in `/gsd-execute-phase` or by a user running\n35\t`/gsd-map-codebase --paths apps/accounting,packages/ui`), the workflow\n36\toperates in **incremental-remap mode**:\n37\t\n38\t- Pass `--paths <p1>,<p2>,...` through to each spawned `gsd-codebase-mapper`\n39\t  agent's prompt. Agents scope their Glob/Grep/Bash exploration to the listed\n40\t  repo-relative prefixes only — no whole-repo scan.\n41\t- Reject path values that contain `..`, start with `/`, or include shell\n42\t  metacharacters (`;`, `` ` ``, `$`, `&`, `|`, `<`, `>`). If all provided\n43\t  paths are invalid, fall back to a normal whole-repo run.\n44\t- The `last_mapped_commit` baseline is NOT the mapper's job. It is stamped\n45\t  deterministically by the `stamp_codebase_map` step below, on every run,\n46\t  incremental or full. See that step for why.\n47\t\n48\t**Explicit contract — propagate `--paths` through a single normalized\n49\tvariable.** Downstream steps (`spawn_agents`, `sequential_mapping`, and any\n50\tAgent-mode prompt construction) MUST use `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` to ensure every\n51\tmapper receives the same deterministic scope. Without this contract\n52\tincremental-remap can silently regress to a whole-repo scan.\n53\t\n54\t```bash\n55\t# Validated, comma-separated paths (empty if --paths absent or all rejected):\n56\tSCOPED_PATHS=\"<validated paths or empty>\"\n57\tif [ -n \"$SCOPED_PATHS\" ]; then\n58\t  PATH_SCOPE_HINT=\"--paths $SCOPED_PATHS\"\n59\telse\n60\t  PATH_SCOPE_HINT=\"\"\n61\tfi\n62\t```\n63\t\n64\tAll mapper prompts built later in this workflow MUST include\n65\t`${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` (expanded to empty when full-repo mode is in effect).\n66\t\n67\tWhen `--paths` is absent, behave exactly as before: full-repo scan, all 7\n68\tdocuments refreshed.\n69\t</step>\n70\t\n71\t<step name=\"init_context\" priority=\"first\">\n72\tLoad codebase mapping context:\n73\t\n74\t```bash\n75\t_GSD_SHIM_NAME=\"gsd-tools.cjs\"; _GSD_RUNTIME_ROOT=\"${RUNTIME_DIR:-$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null || pwd)}\"; GSD_TOOLS=\"${_GSD_RUNTIME_ROOT}/gsd-core/bin/${_GSD_SHIM_NAME}\"; _gsd_at() { for _p; do if [ -f \"$_p\" ]; then GSD_TOOLS=\"$_p\"; return 0; fi; done; return 1; }; _gsd_id_ok() { case \"$(\"$1\" runtime-identity --raw 2>/dev/null || true)\" in '{\"packageName\":\"@opengsd/gsd-core\"'*'}') return 0;; *) return 1;; esac; }; _gsd_homes() { set -- \"${CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.claude}\" \"${ANTIGRAVITY_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.gemini/antigravity}\" \"$HOME/.gemini/antigravity-ide\" \"$HOME/.gemini/antigravity-cli\" \"${AUGMENT_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.augment}\" \"${CLINE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.cline}\" \"${CODEBUDDY_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.codebuddy}\" \"${CODEX_HOME:-$HOME/.codex}\" \"${COPILOT_CONFIG_DIR:-${COPILOT_HOME:-$HOME/.copilot}}\" \"${CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.cursor}\" \"${HERMES_HOME:-$HOME/.hermes}\" \"${KILO_CONFIG_DIR:-${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-$HOME/.config}/kilo}\" \"${KIMI_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.config/agents}\" \"$HOME/.agents\" \"${KIMI_CODE_HOME:-$HOME/.kimi-code}\" \"${OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR:-${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-$HOME/.config}/opencode}\" \"${PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR:-$HOME/.pi/agent}\" \"${QWEN_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.qwen}\" \"${TRAE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.trae}\" \"${WINDSURF_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.codeium/windsurf}\" \"${ZCODE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.zcode}\" \"${GROK_AGENTS_HOME:-$HOME/.agents}\"; for _h; do _gsd_at \"$_h/gsd-core/bin/${_GSD_SHIM_NAME}\" && return 0; done; return 1; }; if _gsd_at \"${_GSD_RUNTIME_ROOT}/gsd-core/bin/${_GSD_SHIM_NAME}\" \"${_GSD_RUNTIME_ROOT}/.claude/gsd-core/bin/${_GSD_SHIM_NAME}\" \"${_GSD_RUNTIME_ROOT}/.codex/gsd-core/bin/${_GSD_SHIM_NAME}\"; then gsd_run() { node \"$GSD_TOOLS\" \"$@\"; }; elif _gsd_homes; then gsd_run() { node \"$GSD_TOOLS\" \"$@\"; }; elif unset -f gsd_run; _G=\"$(command -v gsd_run)\"; [ -n \"$_G\" ] && _gsd_id_ok \"$_G\"; then GSD_TOOLS=\"$_G\"; gsd_run() { \"$GSD_TOOLS\" \"$@\"; }; else echo \"ERROR: gsd-tools.cjs not found at $GSD_TOOLS and no identity-proving gsd_run is on PATH. Run: npx -y @opengsd/gsd-core@latest --claude --local\" >&2; exit 1; fi; GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS=unverified; _gsd_id_ok gsd_run && GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS=ok; export GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS; [ \"$GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS\" = ok ] || echo \"WARNING: \\\"$GSD_TOOLS\\\" did not prove it is @opengsd/gsd-core - it is either a different package or an @opengsd/gsd-core older than the runtime-identity verb. See docs/how-to/diagnose-a-foreign-gsd-tools.md\" >&2; if [ -n \"${CLAUDE_ENV_FILE:-}\" ] && [ -n \"${GSD_TOOLS:-}\" ]; then printf \"export PATH='%s':\\\"\\$PATH\\\"\\n\" \"${GSD_TOOLS%/*}\" >> \"$CLAUDE_ENV_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true; fi\n76\tGSD_WS=$(echo \" $ARGUMENTS\" | sed -nE 's/.* --ws +([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*).*/--ws \\1/p' | head -n 1)\n77\tINIT=$(gsd_run query init.map-codebase ${GSD_WS:+--ws=${GSD_WS##* }})\n78\tif [[ \"$INIT\" == @file:* ]]; then INIT=$(cat \"${INIT#@file:}\"); fi\n79\tAGENT_SKILLS_MAPPER=$(gsd_run query agent-skills gsd-codebase-mapper ${GSD_WS:+--ws=${GSD_WS##* }})\n80\t```\n81\t\n82\tExtract from init JSON: `mapper_model`, `commit_docs`, `codebase_dir`, `existing_maps`, `has_maps`, `codebase_dir_exists`, `subagent_timeout`, `date`.\n83\t</step>\n84\t\n85\t<step name=\"check_existing\">\n86\tCheck if .planning/codebase/ already exists using `has_maps` from init context.\n87\t\n88\tIf `codebase_dir_exists` is true:\n89\t```bash\n90\tls -la .planning/codebase/\n91\t```\n92\t\n93\t**If exists:**\n94\t\n95\t```\n96\t.planning/codebase/ already exists with these documents:\n97\t[List files found]\n98\t\n99\tWhat's next?\n100\t1. Refresh - Delete existing and remap codebase\n101\t2. Update - Keep existing, only update specific documents\n102\t3. Skip - Use existing codebase map as-is\n103\t```\n104\t\n105\tWait for user response.\n106\t\n107\tIf \"Refresh\": Delete .planning/codebase/, continue to create_structure\n108\tIf \"Update\": Ask which documents to update, then record the selection for the\n109\tstamp step below and continue to spawn_agents (filtered):\n110\t\n111\t```bash\n112\t# Comma-separated filenames the user selected, e.g. \"STACK.md,CONCERNS.md\":\n113\tUPDATED_DOCS=\"<selected documents>\"\n114\t```\n115\t\n116\tIf \"Skip\": Exit workflow\n117\t\n118\t`UPDATED_DOCS` narrows `stamp_codebase_map`. Leave it empty on every other\n119\tpath (Refresh, first run, `--paths`), which regenerate all seven documents.\n120\tAn Update run does not touch the documents the user did not select, so\n121\tstamping those at HEAD would claim a freshness they do not have.\n122\t\n123\t**If doesn't exist:**\n124\tContinue to create_structure.\n125\t</step>\n126\t\n127\t<step name=\"create_structure\">\n128\tCreate .planning/codebase/ directory:\n129\t\n130\t```bash\n131\tmkdir -p .planning/codebase\n132\t```\n133\t\n134\t**Expected output files:**\n135\t- STACK.md (from tech mapper)\n136\t- INTEGRATIONS.md (from tech mapper)\n137\t- ARCHITECTURE.md (from arch mapper)\n138\t- STRUCTURE.md (from arch mapper)\n139\t- CONVENTIONS.md (from quality mapper)\n140\t- TESTING.md (from quality mapper)\n141\t- CONCERNS.md (from concerns mapper)\n142\t\n143\tContinue to spawn_agents.\n144\t</step>\n145\t\n146\t<step name=\"detect_runtime_capabilities\">\n147\tBefore spawning agents, detect whether the current runtime supports the `Agent` tool for subagent delegation.\n148\t\n149\t**How to detect:** Check if you have access to an `Agent` tool (may be capitalized as `Agent` or lowercase as `agent` depending on runtime). If you do NOT have an `Agent`/`agent` tool (or only have tools like `browser_subagent` which is for web browsing, NOT code analysis):\n150\t\n151\t→ **Skip `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`** — go directly to `sequential_mapping` instead.\n152\t\n153\t**CRITICAL:** Never use `browser_subagent` or `Explore` as a substitute for `Agent`. The `browser_subagent` tool is exclusively for web page interaction and will fail for codebase analysis. If `Agent` is unavailable, perform the mapping sequentially in-context.\n154\t</step>\n155\t\n156\t<step name=\"spawn_agents\" condition=\"Agent tool is available\">\n157\tSpawn 4 parallel gsd-codebase-mapper agents.\n158\t\n159\t<!-- #2508 runtime-aware-dispatch -->\n160\t\n161\t> **Runtime-aware dispatch (#2508 Phase 4).** GSD workflows dispatch specialized subagents by role. Before dispatching on a built-in-only runtime (kimi-code — three built-ins only), resolve the role to a built-in via `gsd_run query resolve-dispatch-type --requested <role> --raw`. On named-dispatch runtimes (Claude/OpenCode/…) the role is returned unchanged; on kimi-code it maps to `coder`/`explore`/`plan` by role-suffix. The persona rides `${AGENT_SKILLS_<ROLE>}` (Phase 3) regardless. See @gsd-core/references/runtime-aware-dispatch.md.\n162\t\n163\t<!-- #2517 model-omit-on-inherit -->\n164\t\n165\t> **Model omission (#2517).** Omit the `model` parameter entirely when the value it would carry (`mapper_model`) is `\"inherit\"` or empty. An empty value 404s on runtimes without native tier aliases — the default on non-Claude runtimes. Omitting it inherits the orchestrator's model. See @gsd-core/references/model-profile-resolution.md.\n166\t\n167\tUse Agent tool with `subagent_type=\"gsd-codebase-mapper\"`, `model=\"{mapper_model}\"`, and `run_in_background=true` for parallel execution.\n168\t\n169\t**CRITICAL:** Use the dedicated `gsd-codebase-mapper` agent, NOT `Explore` or `browser_subagent`. The mapper agent writes documents directly.\n170\t\n171\tPrint: \"Spawning 4 parallel codebase mapper agents (each runs in a subagent — no output until they return, ~1–5 min; expected, not a freeze)\"\n172\t\n173\t**Agent 1: Tech Focus**\n174\t\n175\t```text\n176\tAgent(\n177\t  subagent_type=\"gsd-codebase-mapper\",\n178\t  model=\"{mapper_model}\",\n179\t  run_in_background=true,\n180\t  description=\"Map codebase tech stack\",\n181\t  prompt=\"Focus: tech\n182\tToday's date: {date}\n183\t\n184\tAnalyze this codebase for technology stack and external integrations.\n185\t\n186\tWrite these documents to {codebase_dir}/:\n187\t- STACK.md - Languages, runtime, frameworks, dependencies, configuration\n188\t- INTEGRATIONS.md - External APIs, databases, auth providers, webhooks\n189\t\n190\tIMPORTANT: Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer `*... analysis: ...*`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) to {date}, overwriting any existing date.\n191\t\n192\tScope: ${PATH_SCOPE_HINT:-(full repo)} — when --paths is supplied, restrict exploration to those prefixes only.\n193\t\n194\tExplore thoroughly. Write documents directly using templates. Return confirmation only.\n195\t${AGENT_SKILLS_MAPPER}\"\n196\t)\n197\t```\n198\t\n199\t**Agent 2: Architecture Focus**\n200\t\n201\t```text\n202\tAgent(\n203\t  subagent_type=\"gsd-codebase-mapper\",\n204\t  model=\"{mapper_model}\",\n205\t  run_in_background=true,\n206\t  description=\"Map codebase architecture\",\n207\t  prompt=\"Focus: arch\n208\tToday's date: {date}\n209\t\n210\tAnalyze this codebase architecture and directory structure.\n211\t\n212\tWrite these documents to {codebase_dir}/:\n213\t- ARCHITECTURE.md - Pattern, layers, data flow, abstractions, entry points\n214\t- STRUCTURE.md - Directory layout, key locations, naming conventions\n215\t\n216\tIMPORTANT: Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer `*... analysis: ...*`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) to {date}, overwriting any existing date.\n217\t\n218\tScope: ${PATH_SCOPE_HINT:-(full repo)} — when --paths is supplied, restrict exploration to those prefixes only.\n219\t\n220\tExplore thoroughly. Write documents directly using templates. Return confirmation only.\n221\t${AGENT_SKILLS_MAPPER}\"\n222\t)\n223\t```\n224\t\n225\t**Agent 3: Quality Focus**\n226\t\n227\t```text\n228\tAgent(\n229\t  subagent_type=\"gsd-codebase-mapper\",\n230\t  model=\"{mapper_model}\",\n231\t  run_in_background=true,\n232\t  description=\"Map codebase conventions\",\n233\t  prompt=\"Focus: quality\n234\tToday's date: {date}\n235\t\n236\tAnalyze this codebase for coding conventions and testing patterns.\n237\t\n238\tWrite these documents to {codebase_dir}/:\n239\t- CONVENTIONS.md - Code style, naming, patterns, error handling\n240\t- TESTING.md - Framework, structure, mocking, coverage\n241\t\n242\tIMPORTANT: Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer `*... analysis: ...*`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) to {date}, overwriting any existing date.\n243\t\n244\tScope: ${PATH_SCOPE_HINT:-(full repo)} — when --paths is supplied, restrict exploration to those prefixes only.\n245\t\n246\tExplore thoroughly. Write documents directly using templates. Return confirmation only.\n247\t${AGENT_SKILLS_MAPPER}\"\n248\t)\n249\t```\n250\t\n251\t**Agent 4: Concerns Focus**\n252\t\n253\t```\n254\tAgent(\n255\t  subagent_type=\"gsd-codebase-mapper\",\n256\t  model=\"{mapper_model}\",\n257\t  run_in_background=true,\n258\t  description=\"Map codebase concerns\",\n259\t  prompt=\"Focus: concerns\n260\tToday's date: {date}\n261\t\n262\tAnalyze this codebase for technical debt, known issues, and areas of concern.\n263\t\n264\tWrite this document to {codebase_dir}/:\n265\t- CONCERNS.md - Tech debt, bugs, security, performance, fragile areas\n266\t\n267\tIMPORTANT: Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer `*... analysis: ...*`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) to {date}, overwriting any existing date.\n268\t\n269\tScope: ${PATH_SCOPE_HINT:-(full repo)} — when --paths is supplied, restrict exploration to those prefixes only.\n270\t\n271\tExplore thoroughly. Write document directly using template. Return confirmation only.\n272\t${AGENT_SKILLS_MAPPER}\"\n273\t)\n274\t```\n275\t\n276\t> **ORCHESTRATOR RULE — CODEX RUNTIME**: After calling all 4 Agent() calls above with `run_in_background=true`, do NOT read any source files, analyze the codebase, or write any mapping documents independently while the subagents are active. Wait for all 4 agents to complete before proceeding to collect_confirmations. This prevents duplicate work and wasted context.\n277\t\n278\tContinue to collect_confirmations.\n279\t</step>\n280\t\n281\t<step name=\"collect_confirmations\">\n282\tWait for all 4 background agents to finish, then read each agent's output file to collect confirmations.\n283\t\n284\tEach `Agent(...)` call above with `run_in_background=true` returns an `async_launched` result that carries an `outputFile` path (and `canReadOutputFile: true`). The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. NOT document contents.\n309\t\n310\tIf any agent failed, note the failure and continue with successful documents.\n311\t\n312\tContinue to verify_output.\n313\t</step>\n314\t\n315\t<step name=\"sequential_mapping\" condition=\"Agent tool is NOT available (e.g. Antigravity, Codex)\">\n316\tWhen the `Agent` tool is unavailable, perform codebase mapping sequentially in the current context. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. Use only file system tools (Read, Bash, Write, Grep, Glob, list_dir, view_file, grep_search, or equivalent tools available in your runtime).\n319\t\n320\t**IMPORTANT:** Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer, `<!-- refreshed -->`) to `{date}` from init context, overwriting any existing date — Update runs seed from files with concrete prior dates, so merely replacing `[YYYY-MM-DD]` placeholders is not sufficient. NEVER guess the date.\n321\t\n322\t**SCOPE:** When `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` is non-empty (i.e. `--paths` was supplied), restrict every pass below to the validated path prefixes in `${SCOPED_PATHS}`. Do NOT scan files outside those prefixes. When `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` is empty, perform a full-repo scan.\n323\t\n324\tPerform all 4 mapping passes sequentially:\n325\t\n326\t**Pass 1: Tech Focus**\n327\t- Explore package.json/Cargo.toml/go.mod/requirements.txt, config files, dependency trees\n328\t- Write `.planning/codebase/STACK.md` — Languages, runtime, frameworks, dependencies, configuration\n329\t- Write `.planning/codebase/INTEGRATIONS.md` — External APIs, databases, auth providers, webhooks\n330\t\n331\t**Pass 2: Architecture Focus**\n332\t- Explore directory structure, entry points, module boundaries, data flow\n333\t- Write `.planning/codebase/ARCHITECTURE.md` — Pattern, layers, data flow, abstractions, entry points\n334\t- Write `.planning/codebase/STRUCTURE.md` — Directory layout, key locations, naming conventions\n335\t\n336\t**Pass 3: Quality Focus**\n337\t- Explore code style, error handling patterns, test files, CI config\n338\t- Write `.planning/codebase/CONVENTIONS.md` — Code style, naming, patterns, error handling\n339\t- Write `.planning/codebase/TESTING.md` — Framework, structure, mocking, coverage\n340\t\n341\t**Pass 4: Concerns Focus**\n342\t- Explore TODOs, known issues, fragile areas, security patterns\n343\t- Write `.planning/codebase/CONCERNS.md` — Tech debt, bugs, security, performance, fragile areas\n344\t\n345\tUse the same document templates as the `gsd-codebase-mapper` agent. Include actual file paths formatted with backticks.\n346\t\n347\tContinue to verify_output.\n348\t</step>\n349\t\n350\t<step name=\"verify_output\">\n351\tVerify all documents created successfully:\n352\t\n353\t```bash\n354\tls -la .planning/codebase/\n355\twc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n356\t```\n357\t\n358\t**Verification checklist:**\n359\t- All 7 documents exist\n360\t- No empty documents (each should have >20 lines)\n361\t\n362\tIf any documents missing or empty, note which agents may have failed.\n363\t\n364\tContinue to stamp_codebase_map.\n365\t</step>\n366\t\n367\t<step name=\"stamp_codebase_map\">\n368\tStamp the drift baseline into every document that was just written:\n369\t\n370\t```bash\n371\tgsd_run stamp-codebase-map ${UPDATED_DOCS:+--files \"$UPDATED_DOCS\"}\n372\t```\n373\t\n374\tThis writes `last_mapped_commit: <HEAD sha>` and `last_mapped_at: <date>` into\n375\tthe YAML frontmatter of each `.planning/codebase/*.md` that exists. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XrRGr8V6XQnsmH6wUerZ26","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set, og image,\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export), robots.txt\n70\t|-- index.html               meta and social tags, inline theme-init script,\n71\t|                            noscript fallback content\n72\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n73\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n74\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n75\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n76\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n77\t`-- src/\n78\t    |-- main.tsx\n79\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n80\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n81\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n82\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n83\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n84\t    |-- components/\n85\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n86\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n87\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored, patched locally, logged in an ADR\n88\t    |-- anim/\n89\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n90\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n91\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n92\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n93\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n94\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n95\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph, TextReveal)\n96\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n97\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n98\t    |-- content/\n99\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n100\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n101\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n102\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n103\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n104\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n105\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n106\t    |-- styles/\n107\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n108\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n109\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n110\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n111\t```\n112\t\n113\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n114\t\n115\t## Data flow\n116\t\n117\tBuild time:\n118\t\n119\t```text\n120\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n121\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n122\t```\n123\t\n124\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n125\t\n126\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n127\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n128\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n129\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n130\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n131\t\n132\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n133\t\n134\t## Content model\n135\t\n136\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n137\t\n138\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n139\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n140\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n141\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n142\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n143\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n144\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n145\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n146\t\n147\tRules:\n148\t\n149\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n150\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n151\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n152\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n153\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n154\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n155\t\n156\t## Design tokens\n157\t\n158\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n159\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n160\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n161\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n162\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n163\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n164\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n165\t\n166\t## Animation layer\n167\t\n168\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n169\t\n170\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n171\t|-------------------|-------|\n172\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n173\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n174\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n175\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n176\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n177\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n178\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n179\t\n180\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n181\t\n182\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n183\t\n184\t## Reduced motion\n185\t\n186\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n187\t\n188\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n189\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n190\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n191\t\n192\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers, and a noscript rule guarantees visibility. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload.\n193\t\n194\t## Assets\n195\t\n196\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n197\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n198\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n199\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n200\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n201\t\n202\t## Build and deploy\n203\t\n204\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n205\t\n206\t## Quality gates\n207\t\n208\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n209\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n210\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n211\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n212\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n213\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n214\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n215\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n216\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n217\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n218\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n219\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n220\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n221\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n222\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n223\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n224\t\n225\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n226\t\n227\t## Stack\n228\t\n229\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n230\t\n231\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n232\t|------------|---------|---------|\n233\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n234\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n235\t| TypeScript | ~6.0.3 | Types |\n236\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | ^6.1.2 | React transform |\n237\t| Tailwind CSS and @tailwindcss/vite | ^4.3.3 | Styling, first-party Vite plugin |\n238\t| motion | ~14.0.0 | Declarative animation, imported from motion/react |\n239\t| @react-spring/web | ~10.1.2 | Spring physics |\n240\t| clsx | ^2.1.1 | Class composition |\n241\t| tailwind-merge | ^3.7.0 | Class conflict resolution |\n242\t| lenis | 1.3.x | Desktop wheel smoothing |\n243\t| vite-imagetools | ^12.0.1 | Image resize and format variants |\n244\t| pdfjs-dist | 6.3.289 (exact, dev only) | Phone-number gate: reads the served PDF's text, links and metadata |\n245\t| axe-core | 4.13.0 (dev only) | Accessibility pass of the visual QA run |\n246\t\n247\tUI glyphs are inline SVG (ADR-0010), Deploy authentication uses GitHub OIDC (`id-token: write`), not a stored secret.\n- No secret values are present in source. The `.env*` files, if any exist locally, are not read by this map.\n\n## Webhooks & Callbacks\n\n**Incoming:**\n- None.\n\n**Outgoing:**\n- None.\n\n## Other Integration Points\n\n**Vendored code:**\n- `src/components/motion-primitives/` contains vendored, locally patched motion primitives, logged in an ADR (see `docs/adr/0007-vendored-motion-primitives.md`).\n\n**Build-time verification tools (not runtime integrations):**\n- pdfjs-dist reads `public/resume.pdf` in `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`.\n- Playwright drives the built site in `tests/e2e/`, with `tests/e2e/global-setup.ts`.\n\n---\n\n*Integration audit: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3423,"cache_read_input_tokens":45590,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3423,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":16,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01MfvAaRhBkp5H3vN7jtsatd","agent_id":"a279da56ae348e40b","session_id":"68a95283-d76b-419a-a0dc-116c583eb9de","uuid":"91d4bfdc-6438-4cca-88dd-47020604f245","timestamp":"2026-11-20T02:15:33.864Z","request_id":"req_011ChDNeqLghWvWpQZzBxsVN","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                  Entry: index.html / 404.html                │\n│                  `src/main.tsx` (createRoot, StrictMode)     │\n└─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘\n                              ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  App shell: `src/app/App.tsx` → `src/app/Providers.tsx`      │\n│  (MotionPreferenceProvider, MotionFeatures, ToastProvider)   │\n└─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘\n                              ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  `src/app/Page.tsx` - only file (`src/sections/*`)                     │\n└───────┬─────────────────────┬───────────────────────┬───────┘\n        ▼                     ▼                       ▼\n┌────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────┐\n│ layout/        │  │ components/ui/     │  │ anim/ (barrel)     │\n│ `src/layout/`  │  │ `src/components/`  │  │ `src/anim/`        │\n└────────────────┘  └────────────────────┘  └────────────────────┘\n        │                     │                       │\n        ▼                     ▼                       ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Shared: `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/`, `src/styles/`             │\n│  Content data: `src/content/`   Assets: `src/assets/`       │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Entry | Mounts React into `#root` in StrictMode, imports the font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App | Composes providers around the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Wraps the tree in motion preference, lazy motion features, and toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Single composition root: pulls content, runs the scroll-spy, renders all sections | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver over section ids, returns `activeId` and `lockTo` | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | `useSyncExternalStore` over `html[data-theme]`; toggle applies and persists | `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Nav | Desktop header nav and mobile pill nav, both driven by `activeId` | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Sections | Presentational blocks that take props and return JSX | `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Experience/Experience.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/About/About.tsx`, `src/sections/Skills/Skills.tsx`, `src/sections/Education/Education.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx` |\n| Projects detail | Project sleeve list and modal dialog for details | `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Animation layer | Only place that imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| Vendored primitives | Locally patched motion primitives, treated as owned code | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n| Toast | Context-based toast queue and presentation | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Content | Typed data modules validated by `satisfies` against the schema | `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, client-only single-page React app with layered folders and props-down composition. No router, no backend, no global store.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One page rendered from one composition root (`src/app/Page.tsx`); there is no route table.\n- Content is data-driven: copy and ordering live in `src/content/*.ts`, components receive props.\n- Three small React contexts only: theme (via `useSyncExternalStore`), motion preference, and toast. Everything else is props (`docs/architecture.md`).\n- Import direction is enforced by oxlint `no-restricted-imports` overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`.\n- Build produces two HTML entries (`index.html`, `404.html`) for GitHub Pages.\n\n## Layers\n\n**App (composition root):**\n- Purpose: wire providers, read content, render page\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/content/`, `src/sections/`, `src/layout/`, `src/anim/`, `src/components/ui/`, `src/hooks/`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx`\n- Note: `Page.tsx` is the only file allowed to import content data (`docs/architecture.md`).\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: one folder per page section; each renders UI from props\n- Location: `src/sections/<Name>/`\n- Contains: section components, plus helper components and types (for example `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`)\n- Depends on: `src/layout/`, `src/components/ui/`, `src/anim/index.ts`, content types from `src/content/schema.ts` (types only)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout:**\n- Purpose: page chrome and reusable structural primitives\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/lib/`, `src/hooks/`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` and sections\n\n**Components (ui and vendored):**\n- Purpose: reusable UI atoms and icons\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `useToast.ts`, `icons/*.tsx` (inline SVG)\n- Depends on: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/lib/`, `src/hooks/`; motion-primitives may import `motion/react` directly\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Animation layer:**\n- Purpose: isolate both animation libraries behind semantic components and hooks\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/` (motion wrappers: `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`, `TextReveal`), `physics/` (react-spring wrappers: `ThemeThumb.tsx`)\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`, the token mirror in `src/styles/`\n- Used by: sections, layout, components, app providers\n\n**Hooks and lib:**\n- Purpose: cross-cutting logic without UI\n- Location: `src/hooks/` and `src/lib/`\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Depends on: browser APIs only\n- Used by: app, layout, components\n\n**Styles:**\n- Purpose: design tokens and global CSS\n- Location: `src/styles/`\n- Contains: `tokens.css` (five role colors per theme plus derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`), `base.css` (resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard), `index.css` (Tailwind import, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping), `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` imports `index.css`; `index.html` runs an inline theme-init script\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: all copy and structured data\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `schema.ts` (all types), one module per section (`profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`), `images.ts` (image registry), `index.ts` (assembles `siteContent`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. `index.html` runs an inline script that sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before first paint (`index.html`).\n2. Browser loads `src/main.tsx`, which imports the Archivo font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then calls `createRoot(...).render(<App />)` (`src/main.tsx`).\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, which mounts `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `MotionFeatures`, and `ToastProvider` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`).\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives the email from `contacts`, and calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. `Page` renders `SiteHeader`, `main` with each section, `Footer`, and `PillNav`. Section props come from `siteContent`.\n\n### Theme toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `useTheme().toggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` writes `data-theme` on `<html>` and persists to `localStorage`.\n3. `useSyncExternalStore` subscribers re-render; the OS scheme and other tabs also feed `subscribeTheme`.\n\n### Scroll-spy and navigation\n\n1. `useActiveSection` observes section ids with IntersectionObserver (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n2. `activeId` feeds `SiteHeader` and `PillNav`; clicks call `lockTo` to hold the active id during smooth scroll.\n\n### Project detail\n\n1. A click or tap in `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` sets local state in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`.\n2. `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` renders the detail overlay with focus handling (see ADR-0006 and ADR-0009 in `docs/adr/`).\n\n### Pointer and physics\n\n- Pointer and touch values from react-spring wrappers (`src/anim/physics/`) are written directly to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n\n**State Management:**\n- Three contexts: theme (external store), motion preference (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`), toast (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`).\n- Local component state for everything else (for example project dialog open state).\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content schema:**\n- Purpose: one type source for all copy and structure\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `Project`, `ExperienceEntry`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, `DateRange`, `Writeup`)\n- Pattern: `satisfies` checks in each content module; tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`\n\n**Animation facade:**\n- Purpose: stop direct library imports in app code\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Pattern: each wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards `className`, and never hides content in its initial CSS state\n\n**Motion tokens:**\n- Purpose: one spring and duration system for both libraries\n- Examples: `src/anim/tokens.ts` (canonical springs, exported in motion and react-spring formats)\n- Pattern: CSS mirror values in `src/styles/tokens.css`\n\n**Theme store:**\n- Purpose: single source of truth for the theme\n- Examples: `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts`\n- Pattern: `useSyncExternalStore` reading `html[data-theme]`\n\n**Scroll-spy:**\n- Purpose: active-section highlight shared by both navs\n- Examples: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Pattern: IntersectionObserver, with a lock during programmatic navigation\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: page load of `index.html` (built output at `/`) or `404.html` for unknown paths on GitHub Pages\n- Responsibilities: mount React, import global styles and font\n\n**HTML entries:**\n- Location: `index.html` (main), `404.html` (notFound), both registered in `vite.config.ts` under `build.rolldownOptions.input`\n- Triggers: direct navigation; GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: static meta/social tags, noscript fallback content, inline theme init\n\n**Build and tooling entries:**\n- `vite.config.ts` - dev server, production build, base path from `VITE_BASE`\n- `scripts/check-*.mjs` - gates run by `npm run verify` (`package.json`)\n- `playwright.config.ts` and `vitest.config.ts` - test runners\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers are used.\n- **Global state:** Module-level state limited to the theme store read from the DOM (`src/lib/theme.ts`) and the animation feature status (`src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`). No server state.\n- **Circular imports:** None documented. Dependencies are meant to point down the layer stack (`docs/architecture.md`).\n- **Import boundaries:** Only `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`. Only `src/app/Page.tsx` may import content data. Enforced in CI by `.oxlintrc.json`.\n- **Static output:** No runtime network calls; the site works from static files and must render content without JavaScript (noscript fallback in `index.html`).\n- **Phone number policy:** A phone number is not a field in the content model and must not appear in served output (checked by `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`).\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing animation libraries from sections or layout\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the single animation facade, bypasses reduced-motion handling, and fails the oxlint boundary rule.\n**Do this instead:** Use the wrapper or hook exported from `src/anim/index.ts`. Add a semantic wrapper under `src/anim/declarative/` or `src/anim/physics/` if none fits.\n\n### Reading content data outside Page\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout module imports from `src/content/` data modules.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the one-composition-root rule; sections become coupled to specific data.\n**Do this instead:** Pass data through props from `src/app/Page.tsx`. Import only types from `src/content/schema.ts`.\n\n### Hardcoded colors in components\n\n**What happens:** A component uses a raw hex, rgb, hsl, or Tailwind default palette class.\n**Why it's wrong:** Fails `scripts/check-colors.mjs` and breaks theming.\n**Do this instead:** Use semantic utilities mapped in `src/styles/index.css` and role tokens from `src/styles/tokens.css`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Static content validated at build time; no runtime error paths for data. UI errors are handled locally.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Typecheck (`tsc -b`) fails on missing content fields, enforced by `satisfies` in `src/content/*.ts`.\n- Content tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` check ids, related ids, alt text, https links, and date order.\n- Clipboard and toast feedback go through `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` and `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None at runtime. Build scripts print to the console.\n**Validation:** Compile-time via TypeScript `satisfies` and build-time gate scripts in `scripts/`.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), focus management in `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, reduced-motion guard in `src/styles/base.css`, axe checks in the QA pass.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2230,"cache_read_input_tokens":49013,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2230,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":16,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01MfvAaRhBkp5H3vN7jtsatd","agent_id":"a279da56ae348e40b","session_id":"68a95283-d76b-419a-a0dc-116c583eb9de","uuid":"80cfa944-f1ce-4b44-a7ee-cad78b6f415e","timestamp":"2026-11-20T02:15:58.683Z","request_id":"req_011ChDNfXMFFPE5orekzgXxD","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

`.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: Pages deploy on main\n- `docs/deploy-runbook.md`: steps, settings, cutover, rollback\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one component per file (for example `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`)\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts` (for example `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`)\n- Content and lib modules: lowercase `.ts` (for example `src/content/profile.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts`)\n- Multi-word non-component modules: kebab-case (for example `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`)\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`, `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`\n- Check scripts: `scripts/check-*.mjs`\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section folders: PascalCase (for example `src/sections/Projects/`)\n- Functional folders: lowercase (for example `src/anim/`, `src/lib/`, `src/hooks/`)\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for anything under `src/` (for example `import { Page } from '@/app/Page'`). Path case must match the file on disk; `scripts/check-path-case.mjs` enforces it.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`\n- Data: a new module in `src/content/` with a type in `src/content/schema.ts`, exported through `src/content/index.ts`\n- Wire into: `src/app/Page.tsx` (only file that reads content) and `src/layout/nav-items.ts` if it needs navigation\n- Tests: `tests/unit/sections.test.tsx` pattern and `tests/e2e/` spec if user-facing\n\n**New UI component (non-section):**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Icons: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx` as inline SVG (no icon package)\n\n**New animation effect:**\n- Declarative (mount, exit, layout, in-view): `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx`\n- Physics (pointer or velocity driven): `src/anim/physics/<Name>.tsx`\n- Export through `src/anim/index.ts`; do not import `motion/react` elsewhere\n\n**New layout piece:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx`\n\n**New hook:**\n- Shared: `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New content field or copy:**\n- Edit the module in `src/content/` and the type in `src/content/schema.ts`. Record copy changes in `.planning/phases/01-decisions-and-documentation/01-CONTENT-INVENTORY.md` (per `docs/architecture.md`).\n\n**New image:**\n- Source: `src/assets/images/<name>.<ext>`, imported as a module and registered in `src/content/images.ts`\n- Output must be AVIF or WebP and carry no EXIF or XMP (`scripts/check-images.mjs`)\n\n**New static file:**\n- Only URL-stable files go in `public/`. Reference with `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n\n**New utility:**\n- Shared helper: `src/lib/<name>.ts`\n\n**New test:**\n- Unit: `tests/unit/<name>.test.ts(x)`\n- E2E: `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`\n\n## Special Directories\n\n**`dist/`:**\n- Purpose: Vite build output, uploaded to GitHub Pages\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (produced by `npm run build` and the deploy workflow)\n\n**`node_modules/`:**\n- Purpose: installed dependencies\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No\n\n**`src/components/motion-primitives/`:**\n- Purpose: vendored third-party source, locally patched\n- Generated: No\n- Committed: Yes\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: planning state and phase artifacts\n- Generated: partly (by GSD tooling)\n- Committed: Yes\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: static files copied verbatim into the build\n- Generated: No\n- Committed: Yes\n\n---\n\n*Structure analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6577,"cache_read_input_tokens":51243,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6577,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":16,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01MfvAaRhBkp5H3vN7jtsatd","agent_id":"a279da56ae348e40b","session_id":"68a95283-d76b-419a-a0dc-116c583eb9de","uuid":"875c59d3-7ba8-4e01-9d69-e784ef215b8c","timestamp":"2026-11-20T02:16:17.670Z","request_id":"req_011ChDNhMcQggfu7T34BaenL","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}
